# ★ Apex Atlas — Sequential Technical Audit — Replit Fresh Run 05 (IN PROGRESS)

**Audit file:** `audits/APEX_ATLAS_SEQUENTIAL_AUDIT_2026-10-17_REPLIT_FRESH_RUN_05.md`  
**Repository:** `https://github.com/2f22vtd4kr-cloud/BigContacts`  
**Branch / source revision:** `main` / `c4ddee7d65040e9ce46b119a8e38481da93e9c6e`  
**Purpose:** Append-only chronological record of setup, run-state reconciliation, canonical UI-equivalent discovery/research, tools, evidence, admissions, and card projection. This file is starred before any new research request.

## Audit boundary

- Record model-visible prompts and structured visible responses as captured by the application, role handoffs, proposed actions, actual tool inputs/results, timestamps, state transitions, failures, and concise operational reasoning.
- Do not reproduce hidden model chain-of-thought or provider-private reasoning. The application logger explicitly omits those fields.
- Never record secret values, credentials, or connection strings. Keep direct personal contact values redacted; retain public source URLs and reproducible search text.
- Never invent people, contacts, evidence, admissions, or cards. Preserve failure/incomplete outcomes truthfully.
- Do not bypass scope, authorization, provenance, admission, active-job, or provider-role controls; do not force a fixed research sequence or card.
- Investigator reassignment must follow the existing Boss-directed durable policy. A token-window rate limit is not a hard-quota signal and does not authorize silent key rotation.
- This audit remains a visible workspace file and is not to be pushed to the public GitHub repository.

## Setup baseline and previous attempts

1. The public repository was imported at `main` revision `c4ddee7d65040e9ce46b119a8e38481da93e9c6e`. Local `main` and `origin/main` match. Five latest GitHub Actions workflows for this revision reported success.
2. Dependencies were installed from the committed lockfile with `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` (596 packages). The initial API workflow attempt failed because starter dependencies were absent; after installation and explicit development-schema initialization the app served the Apex Atlas desk and `/api/healthz` reported `status=ok`, Redis `ok`, and research keys configured.
3. The new development database was initially empty. The documented one-time schema initialization succeeded. Prior-project evidence or record counts are not inferred from this database.
4. Setup issue: `rsync` was unavailable, so repository files were transferred with `tar`. Setup issue: the initial blank checkout's `.git` metadata was replaced by the repository clone. The imported project memory says to preserve Replit-managed Git metadata; this remains an unresolved setup deviation. No commit or push has been made by this audit.
5. Existing Run 04 audit `audits/APEX_ATLAS_SEQUENTIAL_AUDIT_2026-10-17_REPLIT_FRESH_RUN_04.md` is preserved. Its job `e187572a-0608-4ef6-8818-cc18c9f24693` ended `failed/incomplete` at `2026-10-17T11:34:59.306Z`, progress 3/4, with 0 inserted, 0 skipped, and 0 errors. Its four discovery slots yielded no admitted target or card. Do not treat HTTP 202, search results, or model output as a successful run.
6. Earlier audited attempts `84d92f22-2481-434a-9c6c-c68c787d8737` and `4b85fc7c-a239-478c-a455-94baf02ffdee` are also terminal `failed/incomplete`; their recorded development-database persistence is not assumed to be this project's state.
7. Prior analyses identified scope blocks, search leads not being fetched, missing Midwest aliasing, prompt-compaction continuity failures, and unsupported registry IDs. The current source includes regression coverage for the documented prompt/scope fixes. The Run 04 trace also recorded an unsupported `inbiz` registry identifier, structured-output errors, and a Groq token-window 429; that 429 was not evidence of hard quota exhaustion, so no credential rotation is warranted.
8. Read-only checks at 2026-10-17T14:00Z showed both Atlas and case-discovery active-job endpoints idle. Historical job lookups returned only status aggregates in this audit; verbose result/trajectory fields were deliberately not copied.

## Chronological audit

### Sequence 1 — establish this run's audit destination and recover the API workflow

**Observed at:** 2026-10-17T14:00Z and later; exact UTC timestamps will be appended per event.

1. The existing `.replit` and API artifact configuration pointed prompt capture at Run 04's already-used audit. This distinct starred Run 05 file was created before any new model request; both configuration references are being updated to point here.
2. A managed API workflow start at `2026-10-17T14:00:16.392Z` exited with status 1 after invoking `node --enable-source-maps ./dist/index.mjs`. A process started earlier remained alive; `http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/healthz` still returned HTTP 200. The exact startup failure cause is not established; no code change is inferred from this alone.
3. Current read-only state: `GET /api/ingest/job/active/atlas-run` and `.../case-bureau-discovery` each returned `active=false`, `jobId=null`. Historical jobs above were terminal. No new live launch or model request has been issued for Run 05.
4. Remaining pre-launch gates: verify the new effective audit path in the managed API process; recover a single healthy canonical API workflow; run the documented preflight, typecheck, build, and bureau checks; recheck active pointers; then use the UI's default launch body only.

### Sequence 2 — previous-run failure analysis and audit-path correction

**Observed at:** 2026-10-17T14:03:22Z (UTC)

1. Re-read the Run 03 post-run analysis. It classifies the earlier failure as upstream research-execution/prompt-continuity failure, not a card-renderer defect: searches returned leads but no pages were fetched; several actions were blocked before outbound execution; the Midwest alias and lead continuity/control-context gaps were identified. Regression coverage for those fixes is present in current `main`, and the five latest CI workflows on current `HEAD` are green.
2. The Run 04 job `e187572a-0608-4ef6-8818-cc18c9f24693` ended `failed/incomplete` (progress 3/4; no inserted/skipped rows or job errors). Its saved trace reports no admitted target/card; it includes rejected scope pivots and the unsupported registry identifier `inbiz`. The job returned HTTP 400 structured-output failures and a token-type 429; the recorded TPM classification is not a hard-quota signal. No Investigator key was switched.
3. Read-only lookups also confirm jobs `4b85fc7c-a239-478c-a455-94baf02ffdee` and `84d92f22-2481-434a-9c6c-c68c787d8737` are terminal `failed/incomplete`. Both active-job endpoints are idle. Only the compact status/count fields are retained here; verbose result payloads were not copied.
4. The Run 04 audit path was already occupied by that earlier run, so Run 05 uses this distinct file. `verifyAndReplaceDotReplit` and `verifyAndReplaceArtifactToml` both returned success, and the development environment manager now sets `APEX_MODEL_AUDIT_FILE` to this path. Prompt capture remains enabled. The already-running API process still reports the old Run 04 destination until a managed restart; no model call may be made before verifying the restarted process.
5. At the last observed check (`2026-10-17T14:03:22Z`), the canonical workflow was still marked failed even though the older process served HTTP 200 locally. One managed restart is required to synchronize workflow ownership and apply the new audit destination; failure cause remains unproven.

### Sequence 3 — restart and verify the canonical API

**Observed at:** 2026-10-17T14:04:09Z and immediately after (UTC)

1. The managed API workflow restarted successfully and completed its current-revision architecture/build checks. Its workflow status is `RUNNING`.
2. The active API process reports `APEX_MODEL_AUDIT_FILE=audits/APEX_ATLAS_SEQUENTIAL_AUDIT_2026-10-17_REPLIT_FRESH_RUN_05.md` and `APEX_CAPTURE_MODEL_PROMPTS=true`. Both Replit config files and the development environment manager agree on this destination.
3. Direct API health returned HTTP 200 with overall `ok`, Redis `ok` (`160 ms` on this uncached check), and `researchKeysConfigured=true`. Both Atlas and case-discovery active-job endpoints returned `active=false`, `jobId=null`.
4. No model request has been made in Run 05. Preflight, local typecheck/build, and bureau checks remain pending. Build-generated source changes will be inspected before proceeding.

### Sequence 4 — preflight and bureau architecture gates

**Observed at:** 2026-10-17T14:05Z (UTC)

1. `node scripts/replit-preflight.mjs` passed. All 13 requested secret names and platform `DATABASE_URL` were present; the preflight did not read or change any secret values.
2. `pnpm run check:bureau` exited 0. Canonical launch/lock, scope, provenance, cancellation, model-selection, no-force-dig, research-run validator, campaign validator, and the included focused test suites passed. Notable test summaries: 44 focused API tests, 6 research-run-validator tests, and 5 campaign-score tests passed; the research gauntlet validator accepted its 50 grounded-reviewed cases.
3. `git check-ignore` confirmed this Run 05 audit is not ignored. The repository has no unexpected tracked source changes at the last tree inspection; only the intended audit/config changes were present.
4. Root workspace typecheck, full build, and the API unit suite remain pending. No live launch or model request has been issued.

### Sequence 5 — local pre-launch test gates

**Observed at:** 2026-10-17T14:06Z (UTC)

1. Root `pnpm run typecheck` exited 0. Workspace libraries, API, mockup sandbox, and scripts typechecked successfully.
2. `pnpm --dir artifacts/api-server run test` exited 0. API: 178 test files passed, 1 skipped; 1,410 tests passed, 17 skipped. Frontend launch-status contract: 1 file and 18 tests passed. No end-to-end browser/research job was run by this test command.
3. Earlier `pnpm run check:bureau` and provider preflight both exited 0. No failures in these static, architecture, or focused test gates have been observed.
4. The full root workspace build and post-build source-tree check remain pending. A previous live trace's unsupported `inbiz` registry action is being checked against the current action schema before any live launch; no alias or forced registry action will be added without a real configured adapter.
5. Source inspection confirms the current structured action schema enumerates only `getAvailableRegistryIds()`, and the Investigator prompt explicitly forbids inventing unconfigured registry IDs. `inbiz` is not a configured adapter; the prior rejection is the intended fail-closed boundary, not a reason to add a fake alias. If proposed again, record the rejection and let the model choose a different valid action.

Apex's institutional purpose, evidence discipline, autonomy law, and role separation exist before operator case input. Your role purpose exists before discovery/research begins. Operator input supplies case-specific direction; it does not define Apex's identity or mission. Investigator models own research decisions: invent queries, choose pages/tools, pivot, and stop. For discovery, identify a named person before contact work; practical reachability beats fame. Do not default to billionaire/richest-person lists. Primary sources over aggregators.
